- **statusline**: Wires codeArbiter's renderer into `~/.claude/settings.json`, or removes it. - **submodule**: The `repo.submodule.*` namespace: inspect (`list`), clone/checkout (`add` / `update`), and register/sync/unregister submodules. `list` reads a tree-ish (so it is deterministic for a ref); the local write verbs (`init` / `sync` / `deinit`) read the working-tree `.gitmodules` and mutate `.git/config` (and, for `deinit`, the submodule working tree); the network verbs (`add` / `update`) clone a submod ([scolladon/tsgit](https://github.com/scolladon/tsgit/blob/HEAD/docs/use/commands/submodule.md) · ★4)
